      const constructorModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the contractor (or constructor/general contractor/GC) name and address. Look for labels like "CONTRACTOR:", "Constructor:", "Between:", "GC:", or the first party in "BETWEEN [Name] AND [Subcontractor]". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "CONTRACTOR: CHOATE CONSTRUCTION COMPANY ... ADDRESS: 235 MAGRATH DARBY BLVD., SUITE 250 MOUNT PLEASANT, SC 29464" → name "CHOATE CONSTRUCTION COMPANY", address "235 MAGRATH DARBY BLVD., SUITE 250, MOUNT PLEASANT, SC 29464"
- "BETWEEN: NWR CONSTRUCTION LLC (hereinafter “Contractor”) 558 E. Brooklyn Village Avenue, Suite 120 Charlotte, NC 28202" → name "NWR CONSTRUCTION LLC", address "558 E. Brooklyn Village Avenue, Suite 120, Charlotte, NC 28202"
- "Contractor: Western Waterproofing Company of America dba Western Specialty Contractors ... address: 2500 Allen Road S Charlotte, North Carolina 28269" → name "Western Waterproofing Company of America dba Western Specialty Contractors", address "2500 Allen Road S, Charlotte, North Carolina 28269"
Field: "constructor_name": string or "Null", "constructor_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Project name and address module
      const projectModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the project name and address. Look for labels like "PROJECT:", "Project Name:", "Site:", "Location:", or descriptions near "at [Address]" or "for [Project Title]". Extract the full project title and address (street, city, state, zip; combine if multi-line). Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "PROJECT: BLDG 1 - PALMETTO COAST INDUSTRIAL PARK ADDRESS: 8651 WATER TOWER ROAD NORTH MYRTLE BEACH, SC 29568" → name "BLDG 1 - PALMETTO COAST INDUSTRIAL PARK", address "8651 WATER TOWER ROAD, NORTH MYRTLE BEACH, SC 29568"
- "Project: Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement" → name "Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement", address "Null" (if no address)
- "SUNRISE APARTMENTS" (header) → name "SUNRISE APARTMENTS", address "Null"
Field: "project_name": string or "Null", "project_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Owner name and address module
      const ownerModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the owner name and address. Look for labels like "OWNER:", "Owner:", "Client:", or the party in "for [Owner]" or "BETWEEN ... AND [Subcontractor] for [Owner]". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found or ambiguous.
Examples:
- "OWNER: PCIP 1 PARTNERS, LLC ADDRESS: 3105 GLENWOOD AVE., SUITE 105 RALEIGH, NC 27612" → name "PCIP 1 PARTNERS, LLC", address "3105 GLENWOOD AVE., SUITE 105, RALEIGH, NC 27612"
- "Owner: Owner Corp, 789 Oak Ave, Village, CA" → name "Owner Corp", address "789 Oak Ave, Village, CA"
- "for the Owner referred to above" (no explicit) → "Null"
Field: "owner_name": string or "Null", "owner_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Architect name and address module
      const architectModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the architect (or design professional/engineer/AE) name and address. Look for labels like "ARCHITECT:", "Architect/Engineer:", "Design Professional:", or descriptions near "ARCHITECT" or "ENGINEER". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found, as this is often missing.
Examples:
- "ARCHITECT/ENGINEER: WGM DESIGN, LLP ADDRESS: 2907 PROVIDENCE ROAD, SUITE 304 CHARLOTTE, NC 28211" → name "WGM DESIGN, LLP", address "2907 PROVIDENCE ROAD, SUITE 304, CHARLOTTE, NC 28211"
- "Architect: Design Firm, 101 Pine Rd, Hamlet, CA" → name "Design Firm", address "101 Pine Rd, Hamlet, CA"
- If no mention (common in some contracts) → "Null"
Field: "architect_name": string or "Null", "architect_address": string or "Null"`;

      // NEW: Scope of work module (summary, with waterproofing focus)
      const scopeModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the scope of work description. Look for labels like "SCOPE OF WORK:", "Work:", "Description of Work:", or sections describing the subcontractor's tasks (e.g., near "perform certain work" or "labor and materials"). Summarize the waterproofing-specific scope in a concise string (e.g., "Joint seal/caulking on building substrates"). Include key details like materials, substrates, or sequences if present. Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "SCOPE OF WORK (\"Work\"): JOINT SEAL / CAULKING" → "Joint seal / caulking"
- "to perform certain labor and furnish certain materials at Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement" → "Sealant replacement on Gateway Village 800 Building"
- "Installation of membrane systems on concrete substrates" → "Installation of membrane systems on concrete substrates"
- If no specific description (e.g., "as per specs") → "Null"
Field: "scope_of_work": string or "Null"`;

      // Dynamically build prompt (add more modules as we expand, e.g., risks array)
      let prompt = baseInstructions;
      let jsonFields = '';

      if (focus === 'pre-award-essentials') {
        prompt += subcontractNumberModule;
        jsonFields += '"contract_number": string or "Null"';
        prompt += contractAmountModule;
        jsonFields += ', "contract_amount": number or null';
        prompt += constructorModule;
        jsonFields += ', "constructor_name": string or "Null", "constructor_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += projectModule;
        jsonFields += ', "project_name": string or "Null", "project_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += ownerModule;
        jsonFields += ', "owner_name": string or "Null", "owner_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += architectModule;
        jsonFields += ', "architect_name": string or "Null", "architect_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += scopeModule; // NEW: Added for this step
        jsonFields += ', "scope_of_work": string or "Null"';
      } else {
        // Placeholder for other focuses (e.g., 'pds' for structured data)
        prompt += `General parse for ${focus}. Return {"extractedData": {}}.`;
      }

      prompt = prompt.replace('{fields}', `{${jsonFields}}`); // Inject fields into base

      // Append extracted text
      if (extractedText) {
        prompt += `\n\nExtracted Document Text:\n${extractedText}`;
      }

      const completion = await grok.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'grok-4-1-fast-reasoning',
        messages: [
          {
            role: 'user',
            content: prompt,
            attachments: [{ type: 'file', file: { id: file_id } }],
          },
        ],
        max_tokens: 600, // Increased for multi-field
        temperature: 0.4, // Balanced for inference
      });

      const responseContent = completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}';
      console.log('Raw Grok response:', responseContent); // Debug: Check this in terminal
      let parsed;
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(responseContent);
      } catch (jsonError) {
        console.error('JSON parse error:', jsonError, 'Raw:', responseContent);
        parsed = { contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null' };
      }
      results.push(parsed);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Grok parse error:', error);
      results.push({ contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null' });
    }
  }

  return results; // Array of { contract_number: ..., contract_amount: ..., constructor_name: ..., constructor_address: ..., project_name: ..., project_address: ..., owner_name: ..., owner_address: ..., architect_name: ..., architect_address: ..., scope_of_work: ... }
}
typescriptreact
'use client';

import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import UploadZone from "@/components/forms/upload-zone";
import { parseFiles } from "@/lib/ai-parse";
import { useSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';

export default function Phase1A() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [contractNumber, setContractNumber] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [contractAmount, setContractAmount] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [constructorName, setConstructorName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [constructorAddress, setConstructorAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [projectName, setProjectName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [projectAddress, setProjectAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [ownerName, setOwnerName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [ownerAddress, setOwnerAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [architectName, setArchitectName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [architectAddress, setArchitectAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [scopeOfWork, setScopeOfWork] = useState<string | null>(null); // NEW: State for scope
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const supabase = useSupabase();
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setError(null);
    setContractNumber(null);
    setContractAmount(null);
    setConstructorName(null);
    setConstructorAddress(null);
    setProjectName(null);
    setProjectAddress(null);
    setOwnerName(null);
    setOwnerAddress(null);
    setArchitectName(null);
    setArchitectAddress(null);
    setScopeOfWork(null);

    try {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("No session - sign in required.");
      const userId = session.user.id;

      if (acceptedFiles.length === 0) throw new Error("At least the contract PDF is required.");

      const fileUrls: string[] = [];

      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const path = `jobs/user_${userId}/phase1a/${safeName}`;

        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(path, file, { upsert: true, contentType: 'application/pdf' });

        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;

        const { data: { signedUrl } } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .createSignedUrl(path, 3600);
        fileUrls.push(signedUrl);
      }

      const parsed = await parseFiles(fileUrls, { focus: 'pre-award-essentials' });
      const combinedParsed = parsed[0] || { contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null' };
      setContractNumber(combinedParsed.contract_number);
      setContractAmount(combinedParsed.contract_amount);
      setConstructorName(combinedParsed.constructor_name);
      setConstructorAddress(combinedParsed.constructor_address);
      setProjectName(combinedParsed.project_name);
      setProjectAddress(combinedParsed.project_address);
      setOwnerName(combinedParsed.owner_name);
      setOwnerAddress(combinedParsed.owner_address);
      setArchitectName(combinedParsed.architect_name);
      setArchitectAddress(combinedParsed.architect_address);
      setScopeOfWork(combinedParsed.scope_of_work);

      // Upsert with new fields (normalized + JSONB for nested)
      const jobId = uuidv4();
      const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from('jobs').upsert({
        id: jobId,
        owner_id: userId,
        contract_number: combinedParsed.contract_number !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.contract_number : null,
        contract_amount: combinedParsed.contract_amount,
        scope_of_work: combinedParsed.scope_of_work !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.scope_of_work : null, // NEW: Top-level TEXT for scope
        essentials: { // JSONB for constructor, project, owner, architect (and future nested)
          constructor: {
            name: combinedParsed.constructor_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.constructor_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.constructor_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.constructor_address : null
          },
          project: {
            name: combinedParsed.project_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.project_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.project_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.project_address : null
          },
          owner: {
            name: combinedParsed.owner_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.owner_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.owner_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.owner_address : null
          },
          architect: {
            name: combinedParsed.architect_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.architect_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.architect_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.architect_address : null
          }
        }
      });
      if (insertError) throw insertError;

      toast.success('Parse complete! Essentials saved to DB.');
    } catch (error) {
      setError(error.message);
      toast.error('Error: ' + error.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Pre-Award Protection (1A)</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload unexecuted contract (required) for essentials extraction.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />
          {files.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Uploaded Files:</h3>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
                {files.map((file, idx) => (
                  <li key={idx}>{file.name}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {contractNumber !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Subcontract Number:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{contractNumber ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {contractAmount !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Contract Amount:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{contractAmount !== null ? `$${contractAmount.toLocaleString()}` : 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {constructorName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Constructor Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{constructorName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {constructorAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Constructor Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{constructorAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {projectName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Project Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{projectName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {projectAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Project Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{projectAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {ownerName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Owner Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{ownerName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {ownerAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Owner Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{ownerAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {architectName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Architect Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{architectName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {architectAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Architect Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{architectAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {scopeOfWork !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Scope of Work:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{scopeOfWork ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
          <Button onClick={() => setFiles([])} variant="outline">Clear Files</Button>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

Specification Sections") and the referenced Division 07, a more comprehensive summary is: "Waterproofing, air barrier installation, thermal/moisture protection (Division 07), joint seal/caulking, and related labor/materials for Fort Jackson Reception Complex Phase 1, Building 1875, as per referenced specs (including procurement in Division 00, general requirements in Volume 1 of 5, and CLINs 0001-0006 for warehouse, dining facilities, site prep, equipment buildings, and demolition/construction)."

I didn't mention it initially because it's not explicit on page 16 (no direct "air barrier" keyword), and my extraction prioritized concise, non-hallucinated summaries from the text. Implications for Enki Phase 1A Prompt and Risk Detection
I absolutely agree—this is prime "slop" where Enki should warn of ambiguity rather than force a clean summary. In Phase 1's pre-award protection, the app's value is detecting misses like vague scopes (e.g., referenced divisions without explicit waterproofing details), which could lead to risks (e.g., unconfirmed air barrier compatibility with substrates, sequencing dependencies, or VOC compliance). The current scopeModule prompt is conservative (looks for explicit labels/descriptions), so the API likely returns "Null" or partial summaries here, but we can enhance it to detect slop:

Add Ambiguity Warning: Modify to output { "scope_of_work": string or "Null", "scope_ambiguity": array of strings (e.g., ["Scope defined by reference to Division 07—confirm air barrier/substrate details from specs"]) }.
Risk Integration: Tie to a new risks module (array in JSON: e.g., ["Ambiguous scope—potential for unaddressed air barrier mismatches"] for generateFromRisks to create exhibits/To-Dos like "Require GC clarification on Division 07 air barriers").
Modular Refinement: Update scopeModule examples to handle "Referenced Specification Sections:" (infer Division 07 as "waterproofing/air barriers/thermal protection").

Thought for 21s
Yes, I absolutely agree—this is a textbook example of "slop" in contract drafting, where the scope of work is ambiguously defined through broad references (e.g., "Referenced Specification Sections:" pointing to Division 07 without explicit details on tasks like air barriers, joint sealants, or waterproofing integration). The rider on page 16 relies on incorporation by reference to specs (e.g., Division 07 for thermal/moisture protection, which implicitly includes air barriers under 07 27 00), but it's not spelled out, leaving PMs vulnerable to disputes over what "the Work" entails (e.g., substrate prep for air barriers? Sequencing with louvers? VOC compliance?). Enki's value in Phase 1A (pre-award protection) is precisely this: Not trying to "figure out" slop via over-inference, but flagging it as a risk/miss for To-Do generation, exhibits, or PM warnings. This protects profit by enforcing discipline—e.g., "Warn on ambiguous scope; require GC clarification before execution."

Why This is Slop and How Enki Should Handle It
From reviewing page 16 (via the provided text and screenshots, including Clancy & Theys "SCOPE OF WORK ('Work'): JOINT SEAL / CAULKING" and Western "PO 1325.50102003" with vague "perform certain work described in Section B"), the slop manifests as:

Ambiguity by Reference: "Referenced Specification Sections:" lists divisions (e.g., Division 07) without summarizing tasks. This is common in military/GC contracts but risky for subs like waterproofing (e.g., does it include air barrier testing or just installation?).
No Explicit Details: No bullet list of tasks (e.g., "air barrier systems, fluid-applied membrane air barriers, joint sealants at control/expansion joints, louvers, door frames, and masonry" as the API returned in one test)—it's slop because it assumes specs clarify, but if specs are missing/unexecuted, PMs get hit with change orders.
Edge Case Fit: As you said, the API's current output ("Building air barrier systems...") inferred too much; we need warnings like "Scope ambiguous—defined by reference to Division 07; investigate air barrier/joint sealant details."
I agree we don't want the API to "fix" slop with inference (risk of hallucination); instead, detect and warn. This aligns with Enki's blueprint: Parse for misses → flag as risks → generate To-Dos/exhibits (e.g., "Exhibit Clause: Require GC confirmation on ambiguous scope per Division 07 air barriers to mitigate liability.").

How to Prompt the API for Slop Detection Without Inference
To implement this in the scopeModule (modular prompt in ai-actions.ts), we'll add a "scope_quality" field (array of warnings) to the JSON output. The prompt will:

Extract summary if explicit (e.g., "JOINT SEAL / CAULKING").
Flag slop if reference-only (e.g., "Ambiguous—scope by reference to Division 07; confirm air barrier details").
Avoid inference (e.g., don't assume air barriers unless explicit; warn instead).
Updated scopeModule example (integrate into code):

text
Search for explicit scope descriptions (labels like "SCOPE OF WORK:" or "Work:"). Summarize waterproofing-specific tasks if clear (e.g., "Joint seal/caulking"). If defined by reference (e.g., "Referenced Specification Sections:" or "Division 07"), flag as ambiguous slop in scope_quality array (e.g., ["Ambiguous—scope by reference to Division 07; confirm air barrier/joint sealant details"]). Use "Null" if no scope found.
Examples:
- "SCOPE OF WORK (\"Work\"): JOINT SEAL / CAULKING" → summary "Joint seal / caulking", quality []
- "Referenced Specification Sections: DIVISION 07 – THERMAL & MOISTURE PROTECTION" → summary "Null", quality ["Ambiguous—scope by reference to Division 07; confirm air barrier details"]
Field: "scope_of_work": string or "Null", "scope_quality": array of strings (warnings, empty if clear)
This keeps extraction conservative—API warns on slop, feeding generateFromRisks for PM alerts (e.g., toast: "Scope slop detected—review To-Do"). TypeScript
'use server'; // Server-only

import OpenAI from 'openai';
import pdf from 'pdf-parse'; // For reliable text extraction

// Initialize Grok client server-side
const grok = new OpenAI({
  apiKey: process.env.GROK_API_KEY,
  baseURL: 'https://api.x.ai/v1',
});

if (!process.env.GROK_API_KEY) {
  throw new Error('GROK_API_KEY missing from .env.local');
}

export async function parseFilesAction(fileUrls: string[], focus: string = 'default') {
  const results: any[] = [];

  for (const url of fileUrls) {
    try {
      // Fetch PDF from signed URL
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch PDF: ${response.statusText}`);
      const buffer = await response.arrayBuffer();

      // Pre-extract text with pdf-parse for reliability
      let extractedText = '';
      try {
        const pdfData = await pdf(buffer);
        extractedText = pdfData.text.trim(); // Clean up whitespace
        console.log('Extracted PDF text (first 500 chars):', extractedText.substring(0, 500)); // Debug log
      } catch (extractError) {
        console.error('PDF text extraction failed:', extractError);
      }

      // Upload to Grok Files API (keep for OCR/layout)
      const file = new File([buffer], 'document.pdf', { type: 'application/pdf' });
      const uploadedFile = await grok.files.create({
        file,
        purpose: 'attachments',
      });
      const file_id = uploadedFile.id;

      // Modular prompt construction: Base + field modules for easy refinement
      let baseInstructions = `Parse this document for contract essentials and waterproofing-specific risks/misses. Return structured JSON only: {fields}. Use "Null" if not found. Infer from context if ambiguous, and prioritize values near headers or "subcontract" mentions.`;

      // Subcontract number module (refined with PO fallback)
      const subcontractNumberModule = `
Search case-insensitively for variations like "Subcontract Number", "Sub No", "Contract No", "SC Number", "Agreement ID", "Sub ID", or similar. If no subcontract-specific number is found, fallback to purchase order variations like "PO", "Purchase Order", "PO No", "PO Number", "P.O.", or "PO:" as the identifier, particularly in top-right headers or standalone codes. Extract the alphanumeric value nearby (e.g., after colon, space, or in table/header).
Examples:
- "Subcontract Number: SR-039" → "SR-039"
- "Contractor Job Number: ... Subcontract Number: SR-039" → "SR-039" (ignore prefixes)
- "Sub No. ABC-123" → "ABC-123"
- "Contract ID: 456DEF" → "456DEF"
- "SC# 789-GHI" → "789-GHI"
- "in the Subcontract 2023-001" (no label) → "2023-001"
- "PO 1325.50102003" → "1325.50102003"
- "Purchase Order No.: PO-45678" → "PO-45678"
- "P.O. # XYZ-999" → "XYZ-999"
- "PO: 7181-017" → "7181-017"
- "P.O. 071000 -" → "071000"
Field: "contract_number": string or "Null"`;

      // Contract amount module (parses numeric/worded, converts to number)
      const contractAmountModule = `
Search case-insensitively for variations like "Contract Amount", "Subcontract Amount", "Amount", "Sum", "Price", or similar. Extract the value, converting to a plain number (remove $, commas; parse worded like "Forty-Four Thousand" to numeric). If worded and numeric differ, prioritize numeric. Use null if not found.
Examples:
- "Contract Amount: $44,875.00" → 44875
- "Amount: $42,000.00 – Forty-Two Thousand Dollars" → 42000
- "Subcontract Sum: Forty-Four Thousand Eight Hundred Seventy-Five and 00/100 Dollars" → 44875
- "Price: 2023.50" → 2023.5
Field: "contract_amount": number or null`;

      // Constructor (contractor) name and address module
      const constructorModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the contractor (or constructor/general contractor/GC) name and address. Look for labels like "CONTRACTOR:", "Constructor:", "Between:", "GC:", or the first party in "BETWEEN [Name] AND [Subcontractor]". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "CONTRACTOR: CHOATE CONSTRUCTION COMPANY ... ADDRESS: 235 MAGRATH DARBY BLVD., SUITE 250 MOUNT PLEASANT, SC 29464" → name "CHOATE CONSTRUCTION COMPANY", address "235 MAGRATH DARBY BLVD., SUITE 250, MOUNT PLEASANT, SC 29464"
- "BETWEEN: NWR CONSTRUCTION LLC (hereinafter “Contractor”) 558 E. Brooklyn Village Avenue, Suite 120 Charlotte, NC 28202" → name "NWR CONSTRUCTION LLC", address "558 E. Brooklyn Village Avenue, Suite 120, Charlotte, NC 28202"
- "Contractor: Western Waterproofing Company of America dba Western Specialty Contractors ... address: 2500 Allen Road S Charlotte, North Carolina 28269" → name "Western Waterproofing Company of America dba Western Specialty Contractors", address "2500 Allen Road S, Charlotte, North Carolina 28269"
Field: "constructor_name": string or "Null", "constructor_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Project name and address module
      const projectModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the project name and address. Look for labels like "PROJECT:", "Project Name:", "Site:", "Location:", or descriptions near "at [Address]" or "for [Project Title]". Extract the full project title and address (street, city, state, zip; combine if multi-line). Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "PROJECT: BLDG 1 - PALMETTO COAST INDUSTRIAL PARK ADDRESS: 8651 WATER TOWER ROAD NORTH MYRTLE BEACH, SC 29568" → name "BLDG 1 - PALMETTO COAST INDUSTRIAL PARK", address "8651 WATER TOWER ROAD, NORTH MYRTLE BEACH, SC 29568"
- "Project: Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement" → name "Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement", address "Null" (if no address)
- "SUNRISE APARTMENTS" (header) → name "SUNRISE APARTMENTS", address "Null"
Field: "project_name": string or "Null", "project_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Owner name and address module
      const ownerModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the owner name and address. Look for labels like "OWNER:", "Owner:", "Client:", or the party in "for [Owner]" or "BETWEEN ... AND [Subcontractor] for [Owner]". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found or ambiguous.
Examples:
- "OWNER: PCIP 1 PARTNERS, LLC ADDRESS: 3105 GLENWOOD AVE., SUITE 105 RALEIGH, NC 27612" → name "PCIP 1 PARTNERS, LLC", address "3105 GLENWOOD AVE., SUITE 105, RALEIGH, NC 27612"
- "Owner: Owner Corp, 789 Oak Ave, Village, CA" → name "Owner Corp", address "789 Oak Ave, Village, CA"
- "for the Owner referred to above" (no explicit) → "Null"
Field: "owner_name": string or "Null", "owner_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Architect name and address module
      const architectModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the architect (or design professional/engineer/AE) name and address. Look for labels like "ARCHITECT:", "Architect/Engineer:", "Design Professional:", or descriptions near "ARCHITECT" or "ENGINEER". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found, as this is often missing.
Examples:
- "ARCHITECT/ENGINEER: WGM DESIGN, LLP ADDRESS: 2907 PROVIDENCE ROAD, SUITE 304 CHARLOTTE, NC 28211" → name "WGM DESIGN, LLP", address "2907 PROVIDENCE ROAD, SUITE 304, CHARLOTTE, NC 28211"
- "Architect: Design Firm, 101 Pine Rd, Hamlet, CA" → name "Design Firm", address "101 Pine Rd, Hamlet, CA"
- If no mention (common in some contracts) → "Null"
Field: "architect_name": string or "Null", "architect_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Scope of work module
      const scopeModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the scope of work description. Look for labels like "SCOPE OF WORK:", "Work:", "Description of Work:", or sections describing the subcontractor's tasks (e.g., near "perform certain work" or "labor and materials"). Summarize the waterproofing-specific scope in a concise string (e.g., "Joint seal/caulking on building substrates"). Include key details like materials, substrates, or sequences if present. Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "SCOPE OF WORK (\"Work\"): JOINT SEAL / CAULKING" → "Joint seal / caulking"
- "to perform certain labor and furnish certain materials at Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement" → "Sealant replacement on Gateway Village 800 Building"
- "Installation of membrane systems on concrete substrates" → "Installation of membrane systems on concrete substrates"
- If no specific description (e.g., "as per specs") → "Null"
Field: "scope_of_work": string or "Null"`;

      // NEW: Risks module (waterproofing-specific array)
      const risksModule = `
Based on the extracted essentials (e.g., scope, project details), identify waterproofing-specific risks/misses as an array of strings. Focus on: substrate mismatches, VOC compliance (per jurisdiction like CA/FL/SC/NC), sequencing dependencies, lead times >4wks, rate consistency, stock issues, ambiguous scopes (e.g., "slop" by reference to divisions without details). If scope is vague or referenced (e.g., "Division 07"), flag as "Ambiguous scope—confirm details from specs/rider". Use [] if no risks.
Examples:
- Scope "Installation of membrane on wood substrates" → ["Potential substrate mismatch for membrane—confirm compatibility"]
- Referenced "Division 07 – Thermal & Moisture Protection" without details → ["Ambiguous scope by reference to Division 07—confirm air barrier/joint sealant details"]
- Amount mismatches rates → ["Rate inconsistency—verify per exhibits"]
Field: "risks": array of strings`;

      // Dynamically build prompt (add more modules as we expand, e.g., to_do_items from risks)
      let prompt = baseInstructions;
      let jsonFields = '';

      if (focus === 'pre-award-essentials') {
        prompt += subcontractNumberModule;
        jsonFields += '"contract_number": string or "Null"';
        prompt += contractAmountModule;
        jsonFields += ', "contract_amount": number or null';
        prompt += constructorModule;
        jsonFields += ', "constructor_name": string or "Null", "constructor_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += projectModule;
        jsonFields += ', "project_name": string or "Null", "project_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += ownerModule;
        jsonFields += ', "owner_name": string or "Null", "owner_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += architectModule;
        jsonFields += ', "architect_name": string or "Null", "architect_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += scopeModule;
        jsonFields += ', "scope_of_work": string or "Null"';
        prompt += risksModule; // NEW: Added for this step
        jsonFields += ', "risks": array of strings';
      } else {
        // Placeholder for other focuses (e.g., 'pds' for structured data)
        prompt += `General parse for ${focus}. Return {"extractedData": {}}.`;
      }

      prompt = prompt.replace('{fields}', `{${jsonFields}}`); // Inject fields into base

      // Append extracted text
      if (extractedText) {
        prompt += `\n\nExtracted Document Text:\n${extractedText}`;
      }

      const completion = await grok.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'grok-4-1-fast-reasoning',
        messages: [
          {
            role: 'user',
            content: prompt,
            attachments: [{ type: 'file', file: { id: file_id } }],
          },
        ],
        max_tokens: 700, // Increased for risks reasoning
        temperature: 0.5, // Slightly higher for risk inference
      });

      const responseContent = completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}';
      console.log('Raw Grok response:', responseContent); // Debug: Check this in terminal
      let parsed;
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(responseContent);
      } catch (jsonError) {
        console.error('JSON parse error:', jsonError, 'Raw:', responseContent);
        parsed = { contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null', risks: [] };
      }
      results.push(parsed);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Grok parse error:', error);
      results.push({ contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null', risks: [] });
    }
  }

  return results; // Array of { contract_number: ..., contract_amount: ..., constructor_name: ..., constructor_address: ..., project_name: ..., project_address: ..., owner_name: ..., owner_address: ..., architect_name: ..., architect_address: ..., scope_of_work: ..., risks: ... }
}
typescriptreact
'use client';

import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table"; // NEW: For risks table
import UploadZone from "@/components/forms/upload-zone";
import { parseFiles } from "@/lib/ai-parse";
import { generateFromRisks } from "@/lib/ai-generate"; // NEW: For generating exhibits from risks
import { useSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';

export default function Phase1A() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [contractNumber, setContractNumber] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [contractAmount, setContractAmount] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [constructorName, setConstructorName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [constructorAddress, setConstructorAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [projectName, setProjectName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [projectAddress, setProjectAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [ownerName, setOwnerName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [ownerAddress, setOwnerAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [architectName, setArchitectName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [architectAddress, setArchitectAddress] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [scopeOfWork, setScopeOfWork] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]); // NEW: State for risks array
  const [exhibits, setExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]); // NEW: For generated exhibits
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const supabase = useSupabase();
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setError(null);
    setContractNumber(null);
    setContractAmount(null);
    setConstructorName(null);
    setConstructorAddress(null);
    setProjectName(null);
    setProjectAddress(null);
    setOwnerName(null);
    setOwnerAddress(null);
    setArchitectName(null);
    setArchitectAddress(null);
    setScopeOfWork(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setExhibits([]);

    try {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("No session - sign in required.");
      const userId = session.user.id;

      if (acceptedFiles.length === 0) throw new Error("At least the contract PDF is required.");

      const fileUrls: string[] = [];

      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const path = `jobs/user_${userId}/phase1a/${safeName}`;

        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(path, file, { upsert: true, contentType: 'application/pdf' });

        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;

        const { data: { signedUrl } } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .createSignedUrl(path, 3600);
        fileUrls.push(signedUrl);
      }

      const parsed = await parseFiles(fileUrls, { focus: 'pre-award-essentials' });
      const combinedParsed = parsed[0] || { contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null', risks: [] };
      setContractNumber(combinedParsed.contract_number);
      setContractAmount(combinedParsed.contract_amount);
      setConstructorName(combinedParsed.constructor_name);
      setConstructorAddress(combinedParsed.constructor_address);
      setProjectName(combinedParsed.project_name);
      setProjectAddress(combinedParsed.project_address);
      setOwnerName(combinedParsed.owner_name);
      setOwnerAddress(combinedParsed.owner_address);
      setArchitectName(combinedParsed.architect_name);
      setArchitectAddress(combinedParsed.architect_address);
      setScopeOfWork(combinedParsed.scope_of_work);
      setRisks(combinedParsed.risks);

      // NEW: Generate exhibits from risks
      const generatedExhibits = await generateFromRisks(combinedParsed.risks, { type: 'exhibits' });
      setExhibits(generatedExhibits);

      // Upsert with new fields (normalized + JSONB for nested, risks to to_do_items)
      const jobId = uuidv4();
      const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from('jobs').upsert({
        id: jobId,
        owner_id: userId,
        contract_number: combinedParsed.contract_number !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.contract_number : null,
        contract_amount: combinedParsed.contract_amount,
        scope_of_work: combinedParsed.scope_of_work !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.scope_of_work : null,
        to_do_items: combinedParsed.risks.length > 0 ? combinedParsed.risks : null, // NEW: Risks as JSONB array for To-Do
        essentials: { // JSONB for constructor, project, owner, architect
          constructor: {
            name: combinedParsed.constructor_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.constructor_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.constructor_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.constructor_address : null
          },
          project: {
            name: combinedParsed.project_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.project_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.project_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.project_address : null
          },
          owner: {
            name: combinedParsed.owner_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.owner_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.owner_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.owner_address : null
          },
          architect: {
            name: combinedParsed.architect_name !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.architect_name : null,
            address: combinedParsed.architect_address !== 'Null' ? combinedParsed.architect_address : null
          }
        }
      });
      if (insertError) throw insertError;

      toast.success('Parse complete! Essentials and risks saved to DB.');
    } catch (error) {
      setError(error.message);
      toast.error('Error: ' + error.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Pre-Award Protection (1A)</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload unexecuted contract (required) for essentials extraction and risk detection.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />
          {files.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Uploaded Files:</h3>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
                {files.map((file, idx) => (
                  <li key={idx}>{file.name}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {contractNumber !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Subcontract Number:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{contractNumber ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {contractAmount !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Contract Amount:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{contractAmount !== null ? `$${contractAmount.toLocaleString()}` : 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {constructorName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Constructor Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{constructorName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {constructorAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Constructor Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{constructorAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {projectName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Project Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{projectName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {projectAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Project Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{projectAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {ownerName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Owner Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{ownerName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {ownerAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Owner Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{ownerAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {architectName !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Architect Name:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{architectName ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {architectAddress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Architect Address:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{architectAddress ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {scopeOfWork !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Extracted Scope of Work:</h3>
              <Alert variant="default">
                <AlertDescription>{scopeOfWork ?? 'Null'}</AlertDescription>
              </Alert>
            </div>
          )}
          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Detected Risks:</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, idx) => (
                    <TableRow key={idx}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}
          {exhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Generated Exhibits from Risks:</h3>
              {exhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
          <Button onClick={() => setFiles([])} variant="outline">Clear Files</Button>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

TypeScript
'use server'; // Server-only

import OpenAI from 'openai';
import pdf from 'pdf-parse'; // For reliable text extraction

// Initialize Grok client server-side
const grok = new OpenAI({
  apiKey: process.env.GROK_API_KEY,
  baseURL: 'https://api.x.ai/v1',
});

if (!process.env.GROK_API_KEY) {
  throw new Error('GROK_API_KEY missing from .env.local');
}

export async function parseFilesAction(fileUrls: string[], focus: string = 'default') {
  const results: any[] = [];

  for (const url of fileUrls) {
    try {
      // Fetch PDF from signed URL
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch PDF: ${response.statusText}`);
      const buffer = await response.arrayBuffer();

      // Pre-extract text with pdf-parse for reliability
      let extractedText = '';
      try {
        const pdfData = await pdf(buffer);
        extractedText = pdfData.text.trim(); // Clean up whitespace
        console.log('Extracted PDF text (first 500 chars):', extractedText.substring(0, 500)); // Debug log
      } catch (extractError) {
        console.error('PDF text extraction failed:', extractError);
      }

      // Upload to Grok Files API (keep for OCR/layout)
      const file = new File([buffer], 'document.pdf', { type: 'application/pdf' });
      const uploadedFile = await grok.files.create({
        file,
        purpose: 'attachments',
      });
      const file_id = uploadedFile.id;

      // Modular prompt construction: Base + field modules for easy refinement
      let baseInstructions = `Parse this document for contract essentials and waterproofing-specific risks/misses. Return structured JSON only: {fields}. Use "Null" if not found. Infer from context if ambiguous, and prioritize values near headers or "subcontract" mentions.`;

      // Subcontract number module (refined with PO fallback)
      const subcontractNumberModule = `
Search case-insensitively for variations like "Subcontract Number", "Sub No", "Contract No", "SC Number", "Agreement ID", "Sub ID", or similar. If no subcontract-specific number is found, fallback to purchase order variations like "PO", "Purchase Order", "PO No", "PO Number", "P.O.", or "PO:" as the identifier, particularly in top-right headers or standalone codes. Extract the alphanumeric value nearby (e.g., after colon, space, or in table/header).
Examples:
- "Subcontract Number: SR-039" → "SR-039"
- "Contractor Job Number: ... Subcontract Number: SR-039" → "SR-039" (ignore prefixes)
- "Sub No. ABC-123" → "ABC-123"
- "Contract ID: 456DEF" → "456DEF"
- "SC# 789-GHI" → "789-GHI"
- "in the Subcontract 2023-001" (no label) → "2023-001"
- "PO 1325.50102003" → "1325.50102003"
- "Purchase Order No.: PO-45678" → "PO-45678"
- "P.O. # XYZ-999" → "XYZ-999"
- "PO: 7181-017" → "7181-017"
- "P.O. 071000 -" → "071000"
Field: "contract_number": string or "Null"`;

      // Contract amount module (parses numeric/worded, converts to number)
      const contractAmountModule = `
Search case-insensitively for variations like "Contract Amount", "Subcontract Amount", "Amount", "Sum", "Price", or similar. Extract the value, converting to a plain number (remove $, commas; parse worded like "Forty-Four Thousand" to numeric). If worded and numeric differ, prioritize numeric. Use null if not found.
Examples:
- "Contract Amount: $44,875.00" → 44875
- "Amount: $42,000.00 – Forty-Two Thousand Dollars" → 42000
- "Subcontract Sum: Forty-Four Thousand Eight Hundred Seventy-Five and 00/100 Dollars" → 44875
- "Price: 2023.50" → 2023.5
Field: "contract_amount": number or null`;

      // Constructor (contractor) name and address module
      const constructorModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the contractor (or constructor/general contractor/GC) name and address. Look for labels like "CONTRACTOR:", "Constructor:", "Between:", "GC:", or the first party in "BETWEEN [Name] AND [Subcontractor]". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "CONTRACTOR: CHOATE CONSTRUCTION COMPANY ... ADDRESS: 235 MAGRATH DARBY BLVD., SUITE 250 MOUNT PLEASANT, SC 29464" → name "CHOATE CONSTRUCTION COMPANY", address "235 MAGRATH DARBY BLVD., SUITE 250, MOUNT PLEASANT, SC 29464"
- "BETWEEN: NWR CONSTRUCTION LLC (hereinafter “Contractor”) 558 E. Brooklyn Village Avenue, Suite 120 Charlotte, NC 28202" → name "NWR CONSTRUCTION LLC", address "558 E. Brooklyn Village Avenue, Suite 120, Charlotte, NC 28202"
- "Contractor: Western Waterproofing Company of America dba Western Specialty Contractors ... address: 2500 Allen Road S Charlotte, North Carolina 28269" → name "Western Waterproofing Company of America dba Western Specialty Contractors", address "2500 Allen Road S, Charlotte, North Carolina 28269"
Field: "constructor_name": string or "Null", "constructor_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Project name and address module
      const projectModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the project name and address. Look for labels like "PROJECT:", "Project Name:", "Site:", "Location:", or descriptions near "at [Address]" or "for [Project Title]". Extract the full project title and address (street, city, state, zip; combine if multi-line). Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "PROJECT: BLDG 1 - PALMETTO COAST INDUSTRIAL PARK ADDRESS: 8651 WATER TOWER ROAD NORTH MYRTLE BEACH, SC 29468" → name "BLDG 1 - PALMETTO COAST INDUSTRIAL PARK", address "8651 WATER TOWER ROAD, NORTH MYRTLE BEACH, SC 29468"
- "Project: Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement" → name "Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement", address "Null" (if no address)
- "SUNRISE APARTMENTS" (header) → name "SUNRISE APARTMENTS", address "Null"
Field: "project_name": string or "Null", "project_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Owner name and address module
      const ownerModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the owner name and address. Look for labels like "OWNER:", "Owner:", "Client:", or the party in "for [Owner]" or "BETWEEN ... AND [Subcontractor] for [Owner]". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found or ambiguous.
Examples:
- "OWNER: PCIP 1 PARTNERS, LLC ADDRESS: 3105 GLENWOOD AVE., SUITE 105 RALEIGH, NC 27612" → name "PCIP 1 PARTNERS, LLC", address "3105 GLENWOOD AVE., SUITE 105, RALEIGH, NC 27612"
- "Owner: Owner Corp, 789 Oak Ave, Village, CA" → name "Owner Corp", address "789 Oak Ave, Village, CA"
- "for the Owner referred to above" (no explicit) → "Null"
Field: "owner_name": string or "Null", "owner_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Architect name and address module
      const architectModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the architect (or design professional/engineer/AE) name and address. Look for labels like "ARCHITECT:", "Architect/Engineer:", "Design Professional:", or descriptions near "ARCHITECT" or "ENGINEER". Extract the full legal name and address (street, city, state, zip; combine multi-line if needed). Use "Null" if not found, as this is often missing.
Examples:
- "ARCHITECT/ENGINEER: WGM DESIGN, LLP ADDRESS: 2907 PROVIDENCE ROAD, SUITE 304 CHARLOTTE, NC 28211" → name "WGM DESIGN, LLP", address "2907 PROVIDENCE ROAD, SUITE 304, CHARLOTTE, NC 28211"
- "Architect: Design Firm, 101 Pine Rd, Hamlet, CA" → name "Design Firm", address "101 Pine Rd, Hamlet, CA"
- If no mention (common in some contracts) → "Null"
Field: "architect_name": string or "Null", "architect_address": string or "Null"`;

      // Scope of work module
      const scopeModule = `
Search case-insensitively for the scope of work description. Look for labels like "SCOPE OF WORK:", "Work:", "Description of Work:", or sections describing the subcontractor's tasks (e.g., near "perform certain labor" or "labor and materials"). Summarize the waterproofing-specific scope in a concise string (e.g., "Joint seal/caulking on building substrates"). Include key details like materials, substrates, or sequences if present. Use "Null" if not found.
Examples:
- "SCOPE OF WORK (\"Work\"): JOINT SEAL / CAULKING" → "Joint seal / caulking"
- "to perform certain labor and furnish certain materials at Gateway Village 800 Building Sealant Replacement" → "Sealant replacement on Gateway Village 800 Building"
- "Installation of membrane systems on concrete substrates" → "Installation of membrane systems on concrete substrates"
- If no specific description (e.g., "as per specs") → "Null"
Field: "scope_of_work": string or "Null"`;

      // Risks module (waterproofing-specific array, with slop detection)
      const risksModule = `
Based on the extracted essentials (e.g., scope, project details), identify waterproofing-specific risks/misses as an array of strings. Focus on: substrate mismatches, VOC compliance (per jurisdiction like CA/FL/SC/NC), sequencing dependencies, lead times >4wks, rate consistency, stock issues, ambiguous scopes (e.g., "slop" by reference to divisions without details). If scope is vague, reference-only (e.g., "Referenced Specification Sections:" or "Division 07" without explicit tasks), or "Null", flag as "Ambiguous scope slop—confirm details from specs/rider (e.g., air barrier/joint sealant/substrates)". Use [] if no risks.
Examples:
- Scope "Installation of membrane on wood substrates" → ["Potential substrate mismatch for membrane—confirm compatibility"]
- Referenced "Division 07 – Thermal & Moisture Protection" without details → ["Ambiguous scope slop by reference to Division 07—confirm air barrier/joint sealant details, substrates, materials"]
- Amount mismatches rates → ["Rate inconsistency—verify per exhibits"]
Field: "risks": array of strings`;

      // Dynamically build prompt (add more modules as we expand, e.g., to_do_items from risks)
      let prompt = baseInstructions;
      let jsonFields = '';

      if (focus === 'pre-award-essentials') {
        prompt += subcontractNumberModule;
        jsonFields += '"contract_number": string or "Null"';
        prompt += contractAmountModule;
        jsonFields += ', "contract_amount": number or null';
        prompt += constructorModule;
        jsonFields += ', "constructor_name": string or "Null", "constructor_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += projectModule;
        jsonFields += ', "project_name": string or "Null", "project_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += ownerModule;
        jsonFields += ', "owner_name": string or "Null", "owner_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += architectModule;
        jsonFields += ', "architect_name": string or "Null", "architect_address": string or "Null"';
        prompt += scopeModule;
        jsonFields += ', "scope_of_work": string or "Null"';
        prompt += risksModule;
        jsonFields += ', "risks": array of strings';
      } else {
        // Placeholder for other focuses (e.g., 'pds' for structured data)
        prompt += `General parse for ${focus}. Return {"extractedData": {}}.`;
      }

      prompt = prompt.replace('{fields}', `{${jsonFields}}`); // Inject fields into base

      // Append extracted text
      if (extractedText) {
        prompt += `\n\nExtracted Document Text:\n${extractedText}`;
      }

      const completion = await grok.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'grok-4-1-fast-reasoning',
        messages: [
          {
            role: 'user',
            content: prompt,
            attachments: [{ type: 'file', file: { id: file_id } }],
          },
        ],
        max_tokens: 700, // Increased for risks reasoning
        temperature: 0.5, // Slightly higher for risk detection
      });

      const responseContent = completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}';
      console.log('Raw Grok response:', responseContent); // Debug: Check this in terminal
      let parsed;
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(responseContent);
      } catch (jsonError) {
        console.error('JSON parse error:', jsonError, 'Raw:', responseContent);
        parsed = { contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null', risks: [] };
      }
      results.push(parsed);

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Grok parse error:', error);
      results.push({ contract_number: 'Null', contract_amount: null, constructor_name: 'Null', constructor_address: 'Null', project_name: 'Null', project_address: 'Null', owner_name: 'Null', owner_address: 'Null', architect_name: 'Null', architect_address: 'Null', scope_of_work: 'Null', risks: [] });
    }
  }

  return results; // Array of { contract_number: ..., contract_amount: ..., constructor_name: ..., constructor_address: ..., project_name: ..., project_address: ..., owner_name: ..., owner_address: ..., architect_name: ..., architect_address: ..., scope_of_work: ..., risks: ... }
}
2.1s
I did not update with this code. I read the revised risk module, and I think we are going in a wrong direction. The API should just stop and warn if the scope is not structed as a simple list like a bullet point. Just one warning is all that is needed "scope ambiguous" or something like that. Let's keep this simple for now. Until a better model is released, the PM will have to do the inference. What are your thoughts?


Thought for 21s
Yes, I fully agree—we're heading in the wrong direction with the current risk module if it's over-inferring details like "air barrier systems" or "joint sealants" from vague references (e.g., Division 07 in the rider). This is exactly the kind of slop that Enki should flag without trying to "fix" it through inference, as that risks hallucination and defeats the purpose of alerting the PM to investigate. Keeping it simple aligns with Phase 1's goal: Protect profit by detecting misses like ambiguous scopes, then generate basic To-Dos/exhibits (e.g., "Scope ambiguous—confirm details from rider/specs"). Until better models (post-2026 xAI updates) improve contextual reasoning, let the PM handle inference—Enki just warns on slop (e.g., non-bullet lists, references like "Division 07" without explicit tasks). This reduces false positives and keeps the app reliable for waterproofing subs (e.g., flag risks for substrates/VOC only if explicit; otherwise, "ambiguous scope" warning).

Thoughts on Simplification
Detect Slop Simply: Update the risk module to check if scope is "Null" or not a "simple list like a bullet point" (e.g., explicit tasks). Output a single warning like "Scope ambiguous—confirm details from rider/specs" in the risks array if slop detected. No multi-risk breakdown—keep it one item for now.
Modular Fit: Retain the modular structure for easy refinement (e.g., add more warnings later if needed).
UI/DB Impact: Risks array feeds To-Dos (JSONB to_do_items) and generateFromRisks for exhibits. UI shows simple table; if slop flagged, exhibit like "Require GC confirmation on ambiguous scope to mitigate liability."
Edge Cases: For this military sample (page 16 rider slop with "Referenced Specification Sections:" and no bullets, page 23 "ARTICLE 2 THE WORK" vague), API should return risks: ["Scope ambiguous—confirm details from rider/specs"].
Updated Code for ai-actions.ts (Complete File)
Simplified risksModule to single warning on slop.
