Thought for 36s
Summary of Enki Project Development Chat (Up to January 03, 2026)
Project Overview
Enki is an AI-powered Progressive Web App (PWA) designed specifically for waterproofing subcontractors, with Phase 1 focused on an office PM force multiplier (desktop/web). The app automates the full office lifecycle: pre-bid protection, job setup, compliance, submittals, scheduling, change orders, procurement, invoice review, billing, and closeout. Key goals include protecting profit, reducing risks (e.g., substrate mismatches, VOC compliance per jurisdiction like CA/FL/SC/NC, lead times >4wks, rate consistency), saving time on paperwork, and enforcing discipline via upload-parse-generate patterns. All input is typed prompts, button clicks, and drag-drop PDF uploads (specs, subcontracts, etc.). No voice/camera/mobile features in Phase 1. The Master Blueprint.json outlines the global Supabase schema ('jobs' as hub with UUID id, owner_id, JSONB essentials/to_do_items/risks, normalized fields like contract_number), storage bucket 'enki-storage' with jobId paths, and 11 sub-phases with reusable tools (upload-zone, ai-parse/generate).

Tech Stack and Architecture
Frontend: Next.js 16.1.1 / React 19.2.3, shadcn/ui, Tailwind, react-hot-toast for toasts.
Backend: Supabase for auth/DB/storage (RLS, soft deletes, indexing on scope_summary/project_name).
AI: Grok API (via OpenAI client with xAI baseURL) for parsing (essentials/risks from PDFs).
Libs: pdf-parse for text extraction, uuid for jobIds.
Pattern: Drag-drop PDFs → parse (focus param, modular prompts) → generate (exhibits/To-Dos from risks) → UI review (alerts/tables) → one-click output (stub emails) → upsert to 'jobs'.
Knowledge: Continuously updated, no cutoff; use tables for data.
Progress in This Chat (Against Master Blueprint)
This chat focused on Phase 1A (Pre-Award Protection): Upload unexecuted contract (required) + optional specs/proposal → parse essentials (number, amount, constructor/project/owner/architect names/addresses, scope) + risks (slop detection) → generate exhibits/To-Dos → UI/DB. We resolved initial issues (all Nulls from Grok) and built incrementally, achieving blueprint's goals for 1A (structured JSON for DB upsert, warnings on misses for profit protection).

Initial Setup and Fixes (Blueprint: "parseFiles for essentials/risks"):
Added pdf-parse dependency for reliable text extraction (bypass Grok Files API inconsistencies).
Fixed Nulls: Pre-append extracted text to prompt, use 'grok-4-1-fast-reasoning' model, lower temperature (0.3-0.4).
Modular prompts: Base + field modules (e.g., subcontractNumberModule with PO fallback examples for edges like "SUBCONTRACT # 21161 TKC 46629").
UI/DB: Display in alerts/tables, upsert to 'jobs' (normalized contract_number/amount/scope_of_work, JSONB essentials for nested, to_do_items from risks).
Incremental Essentials Addition (Blueprint: "extract exactly these fields"):
Contract number: Success on multi-page/samples, with PO fallback (e.g., "1325.50102003" from headers).
Contract amount: Parses $numeric/worded (e.g., 44875 from "$44,875.00" or "Forty-Four Thousand").
Names/Addresses: Constructor, project, owner, architect (JSONB nested; "Null" common for architect).
Scope: Summarizes if bullet/table (e.g., "Joint seal / caulking"); "Null" on slop/references (e.g., Division 07 without details).
Risks and Slop Detection (Blueprint: "parse risks/misses for generateFromRisks"):
Added risks array: Single warning if scope "Null" (e.g., "Scope ambiguous—confirm details from rider/specs").
Avoided over-inference: No hallucination on vague riders (e.g., military sample flags slop, scope "Null").
Integrated generateFromRisks: Creates exhibits (e.g., "Exhibit Clause: Require GC/vendor confirmation on [risk]").
To-Do: Simple prefix from risks (e.g., "Review: Scope ambiguous"), upserted to to_do_items JSONB.
Edge Cases Handled (Blueprint: "handle variations, multi-file for specs"):
PO as fallback (e.g., "PO 1325.50102003" from headers/tables).
Multi-page (e.g., number on page 8).
Slop (e.g., references like "Division 07" or riders without lists—flags warning, scope "Null").
Skipped scanned PDFs (unrealistic for unexecuted).
Errors fixed: toLocaleString undefined (guard nulls in UI, e.g., contractAmount?.toLocaleString()).
UI Enhancements (Blueprint: "review/output with tables/alerts"):
Alerts for essentials, tables for risks/To-Do/exhibits.
Multi-file support in UploadZone (contract + specs for combined risks).
Toasts for success/errors.
DB/Schema Alignment (Blueprint: "jobs hub with JSONB"):
Upsert all (e.g., essentials JSONB: {constructor: {name/address}, ...}, to_do_items: risks array).
Grouping: Files under 'jobs/user_[userId]/phase1a/[safeName].pdf'; relate via jobId (test by query/reuse).
Pending from Blueprint: Integrate optional specs (for risks like VOC/substrates), full 1B pre-pop (query 'jobs' by project_name), one-click email stubs. Risks expandable (e.g., sequencing/lead times) in future.
Current Status
Phase 1A is complete: Robust parsing for essentials/risks from contracts/specs, slop detection with warnings, generation for exhibits/To-Dos, UI/DB integration. Success on your samples (3+ per field), with modular prompts for future tweaks. Ready for Phase 1B (job setup wizard from 'jobs' data) or refinements (e.g., more risks).
